[*Warhammer 40,000* Commander decks](https://web.archive.org/web/20220916000000/https://magic.wizards.com/products/warhammer-40000-commander), an exciting new *Universes Beyond* collaboration for fans of *Magic* and *Warhammer* alike, hit stores worldwide on October 7, and we have four new Commander decks to show off, filled with powerful legends and all-new cards.

![Tyranid Swarm](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_rme4p288ych.png)

Tyranid Swarm

 

![Tyranid Swarm Collector's Edition](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_5iJ7LoIZGJ.png)

Tyranid Swarm Collector's Edition

![The Ruinous Powers](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_35ah77jt27p.png)

The Ruinous Powers

 

![The Ruinous Powers Collector's Edition](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_0lCaCSTUkY.png)

The Ruinous Powers Collector's Edition

![Necron Dynasties](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_e9b6jnwxp4a.png)

Necron Dynasties

 

![Necron Dynasties Collector's Edition](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_IDPfvRg2yl.png)

Necron Dynasties Collector's Edition

![Forces of the Imperium](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_txds8wj9anw.png)

Forces of the Imperium

 

![Forces of the Imperium Collector's Edition](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/en_U9ojTgTEKs.png)

Forces of the Imperium Collector's Edition

You can check out the cards from the *Warhammer 40,000* Commander decks in the [Card Image Gallery](/en/articles/card-image-gallery/2022/09-16-warhammer-40-000-commander-decks-card-image-gallery). Note that the Collector's Edition versions of *Warhammer 40,000* Commander decks are English-only and include surge foil versions of cards and tokens.

![Surge foil Abaddon the Despoiler](https://media.wizards.com/2022/images/daily/7YwyDbwDrr.jpg)

Look for the *Warhammer 40,000* Commander decks at [your local game store](https://web.archive.org/web/20220916000000/https://locator.wizards.com/), [online through Amazon](https://gtly.to/PPb9Kv0es), and everywhere else you buy your *Magic: The Gathering* products.

***(Editor's Note: The decklists below pull the newest printing of each card automatically from*** [***Gatherer***](https://web.archive.org/web/20220916000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Default.aspx)***, including printings which are not in this product. Decklists are not card-for-card product displays but rather interactive lists of the cards included in each deck. Additionally, card images for new* Warhammer 40,000 *Commander deck cards will appear in decklists at a later time.)***

*   1 Soldier // Vanguard Suppressor token
*   1 Soldier // Arco-Flagellant token
*   2 Astartes Warrior // Robot tokens
*   1 Astartes Warrior // Cherubael token
